end on

public subroutine wf_delete (date ad_day)

public subroutine wf_delete (date ad_day);//删除ai_day的数据
long ll_i,ll_count
//date ldt_begin,ldt_rq
//ldt_begin = Date(Left(em_1.Text,10))
//ldt_rq = ldt_begin
//for ll_i = 1 to ai_day
SELECT count(*) Into:ll_count From mz_gzltj Where zzrq = :ad_day Using sqlca;
IF ll_count > 0 THEN
	DELETE From mz_gzltj Where zzrq = :ad_day ;
	gf_commit_transaction(sqlca)
END IF
//	ldt_rq = relativedate(ldt_begin,ll_i)
//next


end subroutine

public subroutine wf_check (ref datawindow adw_1);long ll_count,ll_i
string ls_ysbm,ls_bc,ls_jzks,ls_jzlb
date ldt_rq
adw_1.SetSort('zzrq,ysbm,bclb')
adw_1.Sort( )
ll_count = adw_1.RowCount( )
FOR ll_i = 1 To ll_count
	//syrc
	IF adw_1.Object.syrc[ll_i] > 0 THEN
		IF ldt_rq = Date(adw_1.Object.zzrq[ll_i]) And ls_ysbm = adw_1.Object.ysbm[ll_i] And ls_bc = adw_1.Object.bclb[ll_i] and ls_jzks =adw_1.Object.jzks[ll_i] THEN//and ls_jzlb =adw_1.Object.jzlb[ll_i]  
			dw_1.Object.syrc[ll_i] = 0
		ELSE
			ls_ysbm = adw_1.Object.ysbm[ll_i]
			ls_bc = adw_1.Object.bclb[ll_i]
			ldt_rq = Date(adw_1.Object.zzrq[ll_i])
			ls_jzks = adw_1.Object.jzks[ll_i]
//			ls_jzlb = adw_1.Object.jzlb[ll_i]
		END IF
	ELSE
		adw_1.Object.syrc[ll_i] = 0
	END IF
//	
NEXT
FOR ll_i = 1 To ll_count
//kyrs,xhrs
	IF adw_1.Object.kyrs[ll_i] > 0 Or adw_1.Object.xhrs[ll_i] > 0 THEN
		IF ldt_rq = Date(adw_1.Object.zzrq[ll_i]) And ls_ysbm = adw_1.Object.ysbm[ll_i] And ls_bc = adw_1.Object.bclb[ll_i] THEN
			adw_1.Object.kyrs[ll_i] = 0
			adw_1.Object.xhrs[ll_i] = 0
		ELSE
			ls_ysbm = adw_1.Object.ysbm[ll_i]
			ls_bc = adw_1.Object.bclb[ll_i]
			ldt_rq = Date(adw_1.Object.zzrq[ll_i])
		END IF
	ELSE
		adw_1.Object.kyrs[ll_i] = 0
		adw_1.Object.xhrs[ll_i] = 0
	END IF
//	
NEXT
FOR ll_i = 1 To ll_count
//ghrc
	IF adw_1.Object.ghrc[ll_i] > 0 THEN
		IF ldt_rq = Date(adw_1.Object.zzrq[ll_i]) And ls_ysbm = adw_1.Object.ysbm[ll_i] And ls_bc = adw_1.Object.bclb[ll_i] THEN
			adw_1.Object.ghrc[ll_i] = 0
		ELSE
			ls_ysbm = adw_1.Object.ysbm[ll_i]
			ls_bc = adw_1.Object.bclb[ll_i]
			ldt_rq = Date(adw_1.Object.zzrq[ll_i])
		END IF
	ELSE
		adw_1.Object.ghrc[ll_i] = 0
	END IF
NEXT
FOR ll_i = 1 To ll_count
//yyrs
	IF adw_1.Object.yyrs[ll_i] > 0 THEN
		IF ldt_rq = Date(adw_1.Object.zzrq[ll_i]) And ls_ysbm = adw_1.Object.ysbm[ll_i] And ls_bc = adw_1.Object.bclb[ll_i] THEN
			adw_1.Object.yyrs[ll_i] = 0
		ELSE
			ls_ysbm = adw_1.Object.ysbm[ll_i]
			ls_bc = adw_1.Object.bclb[ll_i]
			ldt_rq = Date(adw_1.Object.zzrq[ll_i])
		END IF
	ELSE
		adw_1.Object.yyrs[ll_i] = 0
	END IF
//	
NEXT
//csrs
FOR ll_i = 1 To ll_count
	IF adw_1.Object.csrc[ll_i] > 0 THEN
		IF ldt_rq = Date(adw_1.Object.zzrq[ll_i]) And ls_ysbm = adw_1.Object.ysbm[ll_i] And ls_bc = adw_1.Object.bclb[ll_i] THEN
			adw_1.Object.csrc[ll_i] = 0
		ELSE
			ls_ysbm = adw_1.Object.ysbm[ll_i]
			ls_bc = adw_1.Object.bclb[ll_i]
			ldt_rq = Date(adw_1.Object.zzrq[ll_i])
		END IF
	ELSE
		adw_1.Object.csrc[ll_i] = 0
	END IF
	
NEXT
//ypjc
FOR ll_i = 1 To ll_count
	IF adw_1.Object.ypjc[ll_i] > 0 THEN
		IF ldt_rq = Date(adw_1.Object.zzrq[ll_i]) And ls_ysbm = adw_1.Object.ysbm[ll_i] And ls_bc = adw_1.Object.bclb[ll_i] THEN
			adw_1.Object.ypjc[ll_i] = 0
		ELSE
			ls_ysbm = adw_1.Object.ysbm[ll_i]
			ls_bc = adw_1.Object.bclb[ll_i]
			ldt_rq = Date(adw_1.Object.zzrq[ll_i])
		END IF
	ELSE
		adw_1.Object.ypjc[ll_i] = 0
	END IF
//	
NEXT
//rjfy
FOR ll_i = 1 To ll_count
	IF adw_1.Object.rjfy[ll_i] > 0 THEN
		IF ldt_rq = Date(adw_1.Object.zzrq[ll_i]) And ls_ysbm = adw_1.Object.ysbm[ll_i] And ls_bc = adw_1.Object.bclb[ll_i] THEN
			adw_1.Object.rjfy[ll_i] = 0
		ELSE
			ls_ysbm = adw_1.Object.ysbm[ll_i]
			ls_bc = adw_1.Object.bclb[ll_i]
			ldt_rq = Date(adw_1.Object.zzrq[ll_i])
		END IF
	ELSE
		adw_1.Object.rjfy[ll_i] = 0
	END IF
NEXT


FOR ll_i = 1 To ll_count	
	//sycs
	IF adw_1.Object.sycs[ll_i] > 0 THEN
		IF ldt_rq = Date(adw_1.Object.zzrq[ll_i]) And ls_ysbm = adw_1.Object.ysbm[ll_i] And ls_bc = adw_1.Object.bclb[ll_i] and ls_jzks =adw_1.Object.jzks[ll_i] THEN //and ls_jzlb =adw_1.Object.jzlb[ll_i]  
			dw_1.Object.sycs[ll_i] = 0
		ELSE
			ls_ysbm = adw_1.Object.ysbm[ll_i]
			ls_bc = adw_1.Object.bclb[ll_i]
			ldt_rq = Date(adw_1.Object.zzrq[ll_i])
			ls_jzks = adw_1.Object.jzks[ll_i]
			//ls_jzlb = adw_1.Object.jzlb[ll_i]
		END IF

	End if
NEXT
/*//kjyp
FOR ll_i = 1 To ll_count
	IF adw_1.Object.kjypfy[ll_i] > 0 THEN
		IF ldt_rq = Date(adw_1.Object.zzrq[ll_i]) And ls_ysbm = adw_1.Object.ysbm[ll_i] And ls_bc = adw_1.Object.bclb[ll_i] THEN
			adw_1.Object.kjypfy[ll_i] = 0
		ELSE
			ls_ysbm = adw_1.Object.ysbm[ll_i]
			ls_bc = adw_1.Object.bclb[ll_i]
			ldt_rq = Date(adw_1.Object.zzrq[ll_i])
		END IF
	ELSE
		adw_1.Object.kjypfy[ll_i] = 0
	END IF
//	
NEXT */
end subroutine

public subroutine wf_myd ();
Date ldt_day,ldt_rq
DateTime ldt_begin,ldt_end
long ll_count,ll_count_myd,ll_i,ll_find
string ls_ysbm,ls_bc,ls_error
u_datastore lds_dxmyd
lds_dxmyd = Create u_datastore
lds_dxmyd.DataObject = 'd_grtj_myd'
lds_dxmyd.SetTransObject(sqlca)

gf_begin_transaction(sqlca)

ldt_day = Date(Left(em_1.Text,10))
ldt_day = RelativeDate(ldt_day,-30)
//DateTime ldt_myd_max
date ld_myd_b,ld_myd_e
ld_myd_b = Date(Left(em_1.Text,10))
ld_myd_e = Date(Left(em_2.Text,10))
//string ls_hfsj,ls_hfsj_y
//select csz into :ls_hfsj_y from gy_xtcs where xtsb =92 and csmc ='HFSJ-GZL';  //HFSJ-GZL
//select csz into :ls_hfsj from gy_xtcs where xtsb =92 and csmc ='HFSJ';

//	ld_myd_b =date(mid(ls_hfsj_y,1,10))
//	ld_myd_e =date(mid(ls_hfsj,1,10))
//	if daysafter(ld_myd_b,ld_myd_e) >= 3 then
//	else
//		ld_myd_b = Date(Left(em_1.Text,10))
//	end if
	DO WHILE Date(ld_myd_b) <= ld_myd_e
		
		ldt_begin = DateTime(Date(ld_myd_b),Time("00:00:00"))
		ldt_end = DateTime(Date(ld_myd_b),Time("23:59:59"))
		lds_dxmyd.Reset( )
		//dw_3.dbcancel( )
		dw_3.Reset( )
		ll_count_myd = lds_dxmyd.Retrieve('YS',0,ldt_begin,ldt_end )
		//	FOR ll_r = 1 To lds_dxmyd.RowCount()
		//		//		ldc_myd = lds_dxmyd.Object.jhpl[ll_r]
		//		ll_hprs = lds_dxmyd.Object.hps[ll_r]
		//		ll_cprs = lds_dxmyd.Object.cps[ll_r]
		//		ll_cgrs = lds_dxmyd.Object.cgrc[ll_r]
		//		ls_ysbm = lds_dxmyd.Object.zsm[ll_r]
		//		UPDATE mz_gzltj Set hprs = :ll_hprs,cprs = :ll_cprs,cgrs = :ll_cgrs Where zzrq = :ldt_day And ysbm = :ls_ysbm Using sqlca;
		//		if sqlca.sqlcode <> 0 then goto error
		//	NEXT
		ll_count = dw_3.Retrieve(Date(ld_myd_b),Date(ld_myd_b))
		FOR ll_i = 1 To ll_count
			ls_ysbm = dw_3.Object.ysbm[ll_i]
			ll_find = lds_dxmyd.Find( "zsm ='" + ls_ysbm + "'",1, ll_count_myd)
			IF ll_find > 0 THEN
				dw_3.Object.cgrs[ll_i] = lds_dxmyd.Object.cgrc[ll_find]
				dw_3.Object.cprs[ll_i] = lds_dxmyd.Object.cps[ll_find]
				dw_3.Object.hprs[ll_i] = lds_dxmyd.Object.hps[ll_find]
			ELSE
				dw_3.Object.cgrs[ll_i] = 0
				dw_3.Object.cprs[ll_i] = 0
				dw_3.Object.hprs[ll_i] = 0
			END IF
		NEXT
		FOR ll_i = 1 To ll_count
			IF dw_3.Object.cgrs[ll_i] > 0 Or dw_3.Object.cprs[ll_i] > 0 Or dw_3.Object.hprs[ll_i] > 0 THEN
				IF ldt_rq = Date(dw_3.Object.zzrq[ll_i]) And ls_ysbm = dw_3.Object.ysbm[ll_i]  THEN
					dw_3.Object.cgrs[ll_i] = 0
					dw_3.Object.cprs[ll_i] = 0
					dw_3.Object.hprs[ll_i] = 0
				ELSE
					ls_ysbm = dw_3.Object.ysbm[ll_i]
					ls_bc = dw_3.Object.bclb[ll_i]
					ldt_rq = Date(dw_3.Object.zzrq[ll_i])
				END IF
			ELSE
				dw_3.Object.cgrs[ll_i] = 0
				dw_3.Object.cprs[ll_i] = 0
				dw_3.Object.hprs[ll_i] = 0
			END IF
		NEXT
		
		IF dw_3.Update( ) = 1 THEN
//			update gy_xtcs set csz=:ls_hfsj where xtsb =92 and csmc ='HFSJ-GZL';
			if sqlca.sqlcode = 0 then
				gf_commit_transaction(sqlca)
				gf_applog('满意度成功！'+string(ld_myd_b))
			else
				ls_error='满意度回复时间更新失败'
				goto goerror
			end if
			//	
Close(Parent)
			//	MessageBox('提示','统计成功！' +String(Left(em_1.Text,10))+ ',' +String(Left(em_2.Text,10)) )
		ELSE
			goerror:
			ls_error = ls_error + sqlca.SQLErrText
			gf_rollback_transaction(sqlca)
			gf_applog('满意度统计失败：' +ls_error )
		END IF
		ld_myd_b =RelativeDate(Date(ld_myd_b),1)
	LOOP
end subroutine

public function decimal wf_ghxx_et (u_datastore lds_ghxx_et);//儿童工作量，小于18周岁
lds_ghxx_et.SetFilter("jsbz ='1' and not (thbz ='3' and ghlx =80) and brnl <= 18")
lds_ghxx_et.Filter( )

IF lds_ghxx_et.RowCount( ) > 0 THEN
	return lds_ghxx_et.Object.compute_gzl[1]

ELSE
	return 0
END IF
end function

event clicked;//================================================================mz_gzltj.ypjc
mz_gzltj.myd

//更新mz_gzltj，把cdfive和cdthirth标识打上
//============================================================================

Date ld_begin,ld_end
Time lt_sbsj
DateTime ldt_xtdr
long ll_count,ll_pb_count,ll_i,ll_find


ld_begin = Date(Left(em_1.Text,10))
ld_end = Date(Left(em_2.Text,10))

u_datastore lds_pbxx
lds_pbxx = Create u_datastore
lds_pbxx.DataObject = 'd_pbxx_bcsz'
lds_pbxx.SetTransObject(sqlca)
dw_3.settransobject( sqlca)
dw_3.reset()

ll_count = dw_3.Retrieve(ld_begin,ld_end)
ll_pb_count = lds_pbxx.retrieve(ld_begin, ld_end)
string ls_bclb, ls_bclb_txt
for ll_i = 1 to ll_count
	if isnull(dw_3.object.cdbz[ll_i]) or dw_3.object.cdbz[ll_i]='' then
		continue
	end if
	ls_bclb = ''
	ls_bclb_txt = ''
	ls_bclb_txt = dw_3.object.bclb[ll_i]
	ldt_xtdr = dw_3.object.sbsj[ll_i]
	if ls_bclb_txt = '上午' or ls_bclb_txt = '下午' then
		if ls_bclb_txt = '上午' then 
			ls_bclb = '01'
		end if
		if ls_bclb_txt = '下午' then 
			ls_bclb = '02'
		end if
		//去排班信息里面去找这条工作量对应的排班信息,如果找不到，则表示这个工作量不需要统计
		ll_find = lds_pbxx.find( "bc='" + ls_bclb + "' and ysbm='" + dw_3.object.ysbm[ll_i] + "' and string(rq,'yyyy-mm-dd')='" +  string(Date(dw_3.object.zzrq[ll_i]),'yyyy-mm-dd') + "'", 1, ll_pb_count)
		if ll_find > 0 then
			if string(lds_pbxx.object.sbsj[ll_find]) <> '' and not isnull(lds_pbxx.object.sbsj[ll_find]) then
				lt_sbsj = Time(lds_pbxx.object.sbsj[ll_find])
			else
				lt_sbsj = Time(lds_pbxx.object.kssj[ll_find])
			end if
			if string(lt_sbsj) = "00:00:00" then
				lt_sbsj = Time(lds_pbxx.object.kssj[ll_find])
			end if
			IF Time(ldt_xtdr) > RelativeTime(lt_sbsj,359) and Time(ldt_xtdr) <= RelativeTime(lt_sbsj,1859) THEN
				dw_3.Object.cdbz[ll_i] = '1'
				dw_3.Object.cdfive[ll_i] = '1'
				dw_3.Object.cdthirty[ll_i] = '0'
			ELSEIF Time(ldt_xtdr) > RelativeTime(lt_sbsj,1859) then
				dw_3.Object.cdbz[ll_i] = '1'
				dw_3.Object.cdfive[ll_i] = '1'
				dw_3.Object.cdthirty[ll_i] = '1'
			Else
				dw_3.Object.cdfive[ll_i] = '0'
				dw_3.Object.cdthirty[ll_i] = '0'
			END IF
		end if
		
	end if
next
IF dw_3.Update( ) = 1 THEN
	gf_commit_transaction(sqlca)
	gf_applog('数量' + string(ll_count))
ELSE
	gf_rollback_transaction(sqlca)		
END IF
Destroy lds_pbxx
end event

SELECT zsm,sum(FSRC),sum(CGRC),sum(FKRC),sum(HPS),sum(CPS),
       (CASE WHEN sum(CGRC)=0 THEN 		       0          ELSE sum(HPS) END) AS HPS,
       (CASE WHEN sum(CGRC) = 0 THEN              0         ELSE sum(CPS) END) AS CPS,
       (CASE WHEN sum(CGRC) = 0 THEN              0         ELSE sum(FKRC)/sum(CGRC) END) AS FKL 
FROM 
(SELECT QTXT.DXHF_FSXX.ZSM as zsm,         1 as FSRC,          0 as CGRC,         0 as FKRC,         0 as HPS,
        0 as CPS
 FROM QTXT.DXHF_FSXX
 WHERE (DXHF_FSXX.YWLB =   = 'ALL') AND
       (DXHF_FSXX.FWSJ >=  )  AND
       (DXHF_FSXX.FWSJ <=  )
UNION ALL
(SELECT QTXT.DXHF_FSXX.ZSM as zsm,         0 as FSRC,         1 as CGRC,         0 as FKRC,
        0 as HPS,
        0 as CPS
 FROM QTXT.DXHF_FSXX,QTXT.H_SMS_SEND
 WHERE  (H_SMS_SEND.smsid = QTXT.DXHF_FSXX.smsid)  AND
        (DXHF_FSXX.FWSJ >= )  AND
        (DXHF_FSXX.FWSJ <=  ) And
        (DXHF_FSXX.YWLB =   = 'ALL')AND
        H_SMS_SEND.STATEID='2')
UNION ALL
(SELECT QTXT.DXHF_FSXX.ZSM as zsm,         0 AS FSRC,         0 as CGRC,         1 as FKRC,         1 AS HPS,
        0 AS CPS
 FROM QTXT.DXHF_FSXX,  QTXT.DXHF_HFXX
 WHERE (DXHF_FSXX.FSXH = DXHF_HFXX.FSXH)  AND
       (DXHF_FSXX.FWSJ>= )  AND
       (DXHF_FSXX.FWSJ <=  ) AND
       (DXHF_FSXX.YWLB =   = 'ALL') AND
       DXHF_HFXX.HFJG='1')
UNION ALL
(SELECT QTXT.DXHF_FSXX.ZSM as zsm,         0 AS FSRC,         0 as CGRC,         1 as FKRC,         0 AS HPS,
        0 AS CPS
 FROM QTXT.DXHF_FSXX,  QTXT.DXHF_HFXX
 WHERE (DXHF_FSXX.FSXH = DXHF_HFXX.FSXH)  AND
       (DXHF_FSXX.FWSJ>= ND         (DXHF_FSXX.FWSJ <=  ) AND        (DXHF_FSXX.YWLB = 
 = 'ALL') 
       AND (DXHF_HFXX.HFJG<>'1' and DXHF_HFXX.HFJG<>'3'))
UNION ALL
(SELECT QTXT.DXHF_FSXX.ZSM as zsm,         0 AS FSRC,         0 as CGRC,         1 as FKRC,         0 AS HPS,
        1 AS CPS
 FROM QTXT.DXHF_FSXX,  QTXT.DXHF_HFXX
 WHERE (DXHF_FSXX.FSXH = DXHF_HFXX.FSXH)  AND
       (DXHF_FSXX.FWSJ>= ) AND
        (DXHF_FSXX.FWSJ <=  ) AND
       (DXHF_FSXX.YWLB =   = 'ALL') AND
		 DXHF_HFXX.HFJG='3')
 ) 
Group By zsm
HAVING sum(CGRC) >= 
1073741824

 column=(type=number updatewhereclause=yes name=cps dbname="compute_0006" )

 retrieve="SELECT zsm,sum(FSRC),sum(CGRC),sum(FKRC),sum(HPS),sum(CPS),        (CASE WHEN sum(CGRC)=0 THEN 		       0          ELSE sum(HPS)/sum(CGRC) END) AS HPL,        (CASE WHEN sum(CGRC) = 0 THEN
             0
        ELSE sum(CPS)/sum(CGRC) END) AS CPL,
       (CASE WHEN sum(CGRC) = 0 THEN              0         ELSE sum(FKRC)/sum(CGRC) END) AS FKL 
FROM 
(SELECT QTXT.DXHF_FSXX.ZSM as zsm,         1 as FSRC,          0 as CGRC,         0 as FKRC,         0 as HPS,
        0 as CPS
 FROM QTXT.DXHF_FSXX
 WHERE (DXHF_FSXX.YWLB = :as_ywlb or :as_ywlb = 'ALL') AND
       (DXHF_FSXX.FWSJ >= :adt_start)  AND
       (DXHF_FSXX.FWSJ <= :adt_end)
UNION ALL
(SELECT QTXT.DXHF_FSXX.ZSM as zsm,         0 as FSRC,         1 as CGRC,         0 as FKRC,
        0 as HPS,
        0 as CPS
 FROM QTXT.DXHF_FSXX,QTXT.H_SMS_SEND
 WHERE  (H_SMS_SEND.smsid = QTXT.DXHF_FSXX.smsid)  AND
        (DXHF_FSXX.FWSJ >=:adt_start)  AND
        (DXHF_FSXX.FWSJ <= :adt_end) And
        (DXHF_FSXX.YWLB = :as_ywlb or :as_ywlb = 'ALL')AND
        H_SMS_SEND.STATEID='2')
UNION ALL
(SELECT QTXT.DXHF_FSXX.ZSM as zsm,         0 AS FSRC,         0 as CGRC,         1 as FKRC,         1 AS HPS,
        0 AS CPS
 FROM QTXT.DXHF_FSXX,  QTXT.DXHF_HFXX
 WHERE (DXHF_FSXX.FSXH = DXHF_HFXX.FSXH)  AND
       (DXHF_FSXX.FWSJ>=:adt_start)  AND
       (DXHF_FSXX.FWSJ <= :adt_end) AND
       (DXHF_FSXX.YWLB = :as_ywlb or :as_ywlb = 'ALL') AND
       DXHF_HFXX.HFJG='1')
UNION ALL
(SELECT QTXT.DXHF_FSXX.ZSM as zsm,         0 AS FSRC,         0 as CGRC,         1 as FKRC,         0 AS HPS,
        0 AS CPS
 FROM QTXT.DXHF_FSXX,  QTXT.DXHF_HFXX
 WHERE (DXHF_FSXX.FSXH = DXHF_HFXX.FSXH)  AND
       (DXHF_FSXX.FWSJ>=:adt_start)  AND
        (DXHF_FSXX.FWSJ <= :adt_end) AND
       (DXHF_FSXX.YWLB = :as_ywlb or :as_ywlb = 'ALL') 
       AND (DXHF_HFXX.HFJG<>'1' and DXHF_HFXX.HFJG<>'3'))
UNION ALL
(SELECT QTXT.DXHF_FSXX.ZSM as zsm,         0 AS FSRC,         0 as CGRC,         1 as FKRC,         0 AS HPS,
        1 AS CPS
 FROM QTXT.DXHF_FSXX,  QTXT.DXHF_HFXX
 WHERE (DXHF_FSXX.FSXH = DXHF_HFXX.FSXH)  AND
       (DXHF_FSXX.FWSJ>=:adt_start) AND
        (DXHF_FSXX.FWSJ <= :adt_end) AND
       (DXHF_FSXX.YWLB = :as_ywlb or :as_ywlb = 'ALL') AND
		 DXHF_HFXX.HFJG='3')
 ) 
Group By zsm

sum(  ghrc  for all )
:adt_begin
  SELECT count(MZ_GHXX.GHLSH) ghrc,    
         to_char(MZ_GHXX.GHRQ,'yyyy.mm.dd') ghrq,     
         nvl(MZ_GHXX.YSBM,MZ_GHXX.JZYS) AS YSBM
    FROM MZ_GHXX
	where (MZ_GHXX.GHRQ >=   AND 	MZ_GHXX.GHRQ <=  ) AND
	nvl(MZ_GHXX.YSBM,MZ_GHXX.JZYS) = 
 and
	MZ_GHXX.THBZ NOT IN ('s','t','r','i') and
	jsbz ='1'
group by to_char(MZ_GHXX.GHRQ,'yyyy.mm.dd'),     
        nvl(MZ_GHXX.YSBM,MZ_GHXX.JZYS)

column(band=detail :adt_begin mz_dz01.ysbm Select Count(Distinct brbm) As syrc,ysbm  From mz_dz01
 Where 
 czsj >= 
 and
czsj <= 
 and
kplb = 4 and
clbz >=1 
group by ysbm
Mz Cfk Ksbm
Mz Cfk Kmz_cfk.ksbm
Select Count(Distinct mz_dz01.cflsh) syrc, mz_cfk.ksbm
From mz_cfk,mz_dz01
 Where mz_dz01.cflsh  = mz_cfk.cflsh 
And mz_dz01.ysbm = mz_cfk.ysbm 
and mz_dz01.ysbm = 

and mz_cfk.ksbm = 

And  mz_dz01.czsj >= 

And  mz_dz01.czsj <= 

And kplb = 4 and clbz>=1
and mz_cfk.fybz in ('f','a','b','t')
Group By mz_cfk.ksbm
		ls_ysbm = lds_jzrc.Object.ysbm[ll_i]
			ls_jzlb = lds_jzrc.Object.jzlb[ll_i]			
			ll_find = dw_3.Find( "ysbm='" + ls_ysbm +"' and jzlb ='" + ls_jzlb + "' and (bclb ='下午' or bclb ='其他下')", 1, ll_count_dw3)
			IF ll_find > 0 THEN
				dw_3.Object.xwjzrc[ll_find] = dw_3.Object.xwjzrc[ll_find] + 1
			END IF
		NEXT
	END IF
	ldt_day = RelativeDate(Date(ldt_day),1)
	IF dw_3.Update( ) = 1 THEN
		gf_commit_transaction(sqlca)
		gf_applog('调整成功' + string(ldt_end))
	ELSE
		gf_rollback_transaction(sqlca)	
		gf_applog('调整失败'+ string(ldt_end))
		exit
	END IF
	
NEXT

lds_syrc_new = Create u_datastore
lds_syrc_new.DataObject = 'd_syrc_jzks'
lds_syrc_new.SetTransObject(sqlca)
//超时提成 2014.06.17
lds_mzks_kz = Create u_datastore
lds_mzks_kz.DataObject = 'd_mzks_kz'
lds_mzks_kz.SetTransObject(sqlca)
ll_count_ks = lds_mzks_kz.Retrieve( )
lds_ghxx_cs.DataObject = "d_ghxx"
lds_ghxx_cs.SetTransObject(sqlca)
//限号人数 2014.11.24
lds_pbxx = Create u_datastore
lds_pbxx.DataObject = 'd_pbxx_all'
lds_pbxx.SetTransObject(sqlca)
lds_ghxx_jzlb = Create u_datastore
lds_ghxx_jzlb.DataObject = 'd_ghxx_jzlb'
lds_ghxx_jzlb.SetTransObject(sqlca)
lds_ghxx_jzlbxw = Create u_datastore
lds_ghxx_jzlbxw.DataObject = 'd_ghxx_jzlb'
lds_ghxx_jzlbxw.SetTransObject(sqlca)
dw_1.Reset( )
dw_2.Reset( )

ll_day = DaysAfter(Date(Left(em_1.Text,10)),Date(Left(em_2.Text,10)))
IF ll_day < 0 THEN ll_day = 0
ldt_day = Date(Left(em_1.Text,10))

FOR ll_j = 1 To ll_day + 1
	gf_applog(String(ldt_day))
	//上班间隔
	IF Month(ldt_day) >= ii_xl_ks And Month(ldt_day) <= ii_xl_js THEN //夏令  ,不开展夏令时设成》7，《6
		ldc_sw = 230 //240 2014.06.17 modify //4
		ldc_xw = 170 //180 2014.06.17 modify//3     //改到1小时 2017.09.12 新需求
		ldt_swsj = DateTime(ldt_day,Time("06:30:00"))
		ldt_xwsj = DateTime(ldt_day,Time("13:00:00"))
		ldt_end = DateTime(ldt_day,Time("13:39:59"))
		
		ldt_begin1 = DateTime(ldt_day,Time("13:40:00"))
	ELSE //冬令
		ldc_sw = 200 //210 //3.5
		ldc_xw = 200 //210 //3.5   //改到1小时 2017.09.12 新需求
		ldt_swsj = DateTime(ldt_day,Time("07:00:00"))
		ldt_xwsj = DateTime(ldt_day,Time("12:30:00"))
		ldt_end = DateTime(ldt_day,Time("13:09:59"))
		
		ldt_begin1 = DateTime(ldt_day,Time("13:10:00"))
	END IF
	ldt_begin = DateTime(ldt_day,Time("00:00:00"))
	//	ldt_end = DateTime(ldt_day,Time("13:59:59"))
	//	
	//	ldt_begin1 = DateTime(ldt_day,Time("14:00:00"))
	ldt_end1 = DateTime(ldt_day,Time("23:59:59"))
	ldt_rq = Date(ldt_begin1)
	//限号人数
	ll_count_xh = lds_pbxx.Retrieve(ldt_day)
	IF ll_count_xh <= 0 THEN
		IF IsValid(gn_rtx) THEN
			gn_rtx.uf_sendmsg(gs_rtxserver,'609048',	'限号人数', sqlca.SQLErrText)
		END IF
	END IF
	wf_delete(ldt_day)
	
	
	//
	ll_count_sw = lds_sycs_sw.Retrieve(ldt_begin,ldt_end)
	ll_count_xw = lds_sycs_xw.Retrieve(ldt_begin1,ldt_end1)
	ll_jzlb_sw = lds_ghxx_jzlb.Retrieve(ldt_begin,ldt_end)
	ll_jzlb_xw = lds_ghxx_jzlbxw.Retrieve(ldt_begin1,ldt_end1)
	dw_2.Retrieve(ldt_begin,ldt_end)
	
	ll_count_cfk = lds_ypfy.Retrieve(ldt_begin,ldt_end)
	ll_count_fy = lds_fyxx.Retrieve(ldt_begin,ldt_end)
	// add by Wcs 抗菌药物费用
	//	ll_count_kjyp = lds_kjypxx.retrieve(ldt_begin,ldt_end)
	
	ll_count = dw_2.RowCount( )
		
	FOR ll_i = 1 To ll_count
		ls_bc = ''
		ls_ysbm = dw_2.Object.ysbm[ll_i]
		ls_jzks = dw_2.Object.jzks[ll_i]
		ls_jzlb = dw_2.Object.jzlb[ll_i]
		ll_zlxz = dw_2.Object.zlxz[ll_i]
		ll_pbcount = ids_pbxx.Retrieve(ldt_rq,ls_ysbm)
		IF ls_ysbm = '185010' THEN DebugBreak()
		ll_row = dw_1.InsertRow(0)
		dw_1.Object.zzrq[ll_row] = ldt_rq //Date(dw_2.Object.jzsj[ll_i])
		//		dw_1.Object.yybz[ll_row] = ls_yq //dw_2.Object.yybz[ll_i]
		
		dw_1.Object.jzks[ll_row] = ls_jzks
		dw_1.Object.ysbm[ll_row] = ls_ysbm
		dw_1.Object.zlxz[ll_row] = ll_zlxz
		
		//		SELECT Min(jlsj) Into :ldt_xtdr From ss_xtrz Where xtsb = 10 And czgh = :ls_ysbm And rzlx = 'XTDR' And jlsj >= :ldt_swsj And jlsj < :ldt_end;
		SELECT Min(jlsj) INTO :ldt_xtdr FROM ss_xtrz a ,mz_jqm b WHERE a.jqm = b.jqh AND czgh = :ls_ysbm
			And rzlx = 'XTDR' And jlsj >= :ldt_swsj And jlsj < :ldt_end And b.ServerName Is Not Null And b.Id Is Not Null;
			
		IF IsNull(ldt_xtdr) THEN
			SELECT Min(jlsj) Into :ldt_xtdr From ss_xtrz Where xtsb = 10 And czgh = :ls_ysbm And rzlx = 'XTDR' And jlsj >= :ldt_swsj And jlsj < :ldt_end;
		END IF
		SELECT MAX(jlsj) INTO :ldt_xttc FROM ss_xtrz a ,mz_jqm b WHERE a.jqm = b.jqh AND czgh = :ls_ysbm
			And rzlx = 'XTTC' And jlsj >= :ldt_swsj And jlsj < :ldt_end And b.ServerName Is Not Null And b.Id Is Not Null;
		IF IsNull(ldt_xttc) THEN
			SELECT Max(jlsj) Into :ldt_xttc From ss_xtrz Where xtsb = 10 And czgh = :ls_ysbm And rzlx = 'XTTC' And jlsj >= :ldt_swsj And jlsj < :ldt_end;
		END IF
		dw_1.Object.SBSJ[ll_row] = ldt_xtdr
		dw_1.Object.xbsj[ll_row] = ldt_xttc
		
		dw_1.Object.jzlb[ll_row] = ls_jzlb
		lds_ghxx.SetFilter("")
		lds_ghxx.Filter( )
		ll_count_jl = lds_ghxx.Retrieve(ldt_begin,ldt_end,ls_ysbm,ls_jzlb,ls_jzks,ll_zlxz)
		ll_count_et = lds_ghxx_et.Retrieve(ldt_begin,ldt_end,ls_ysbm,ls_jzlb,ls_jzks,ll_zlxz)
		ll_count_gh = lds_gh.Retrieve(ldt_begin,ldt_end,ls_ysbm) //ghrc
		IF ll_count_gh > 0 THEN
			dw_1.Object.ghrc[ll_row] = lds_gh.Object.ghrc[1]
		ELSE
			dw_1.Object.ghrc[ll_row] = 0
		END IF
		//限号人数，固定班挂号人数
		ll_find = lds_pbxx.Find("ysbm ='" + ls_ysbm + "' and ksbm ='" + ls_jzks + "' and bc ='01' and zzlb ='" + ls_jzlb + "'", 1, ll_count_xh)
		IF ll_find > 0 THEN
			dw_1.Object.xhrs[ll_row] = lds_pbxx.Object.xhrs[ll_find]
			IF Date(ldt_rq) = Date(lds_pbxx.Object.czsj[ll_find]) THEN
				dw_1.Object.ghrcgd[ll_row] = 0
			ELSE
				ll_find_jzlb = lds_ghxx_jzlb.Find("ysbm='" + ls_ysbm+ "' and jzks ='" + ls_jzks + "' and hzlb ='" + ls_jzlb + "'", 1, ll_jzlb_sw)
				IF ll_find_jzlb > 0 THEN
					dw_1.Object.ghrcgd[ll_row] = lds_ghxx_jzlb.Object.ghrc[ll_find_jzlb]
				ELSE
					dw_1.Object.ghrcgd[ll_row] = 0
				END IF
			END IF
		END IF
		DebugBreak()
		
		IF ll_count_jl > 0 THEN
			dw_1.Object.swjzrc[ll_row] = lds_ghxx.Object.compute_jzrs[1]
			
			dw_1.Object.szsj[ll_row] = lds_ghxx.Object.compute_minjzsj[1]
			dw_1.Object.mzsj[ll_row] = lds_ghxx.Object.compute_maxjzsj[1]
			
			lds_ghxx.SetFilter("jsbz ='1' and not (thbz ='3' and ghlx =80)")
			lds_ghxx.Filter( )
			ll_ghrs = lds_ghxx.RowCount( )
			
			IF ll_ghrs > 0 THEN
				dw_1.Object.gzl[ll_row] = lds_ghxx.Object.compute_gzl[1]
				//				dw_1.Object.ghrc[ll_row] = lds_ghxx.Object.compute_jzrs[1]
			ELSE
				dw_1.Object.gzl[ll_row] = 0
			END IF
			//儿童工作量，小于18周岁
			dw_1.Object.etgzl[ll_row] = wf_ghxx_et(lds_ghxx_et)
			//			lds_ghxx.SetFilter("")
			//			lds_ghxx.Filter( )
			IF ls_jzks = '9406' THEN //方便门诊
				lds_ghxx.SetFilter("jsbz ='1' and thbz ='3' and ghlx =80")
				lds_ghxx.Filter( )
			ELSE
				lds_ghxx.SetFilter("thbz ='3' and ghlx =80")
				lds_ghxx.Filter( )
			END IF
			ll_mfjz = lds_ghxx.RowCount( )
			//			IF lds_ghxx.RowCount( ) > 0 THEN
			//				ll_mfjz = lds_ghxx.Object.compute_jzrs[1]
			//				ldc_mfjz_gzl = lds_ghxx.Object.compute_gzl[1]
			//				dw_1.Object.gzl[ll_row] = dw_1.Object.gzl[ll_row] - ldc_mfjz_gzl
			//				dw_1.Object.ghrc[ll_row] = dw_1.Object.ghrc[ll_row] - ll_mfjz
			//			END IF
			dw_1.Object.mfjz[ll_row] = ll_mfjz
			
			//			lds_ghxx.SetFilter("")
			//			lds_ghxx.Filter( )
			//			ll_count_jl = lds_syxx.Retrieve(ldt_begin,ldt_end,ls_ysbm)     //注释日期 2012.07.06
			//			IF ll_count_jl > 0 THEN
			//				dw_1.Object.syrc[ll_row] = lds_syxx.Object.syrc[ll_count_jl]
			//			ELSE
			//				dw_1.Object.syrc[ll_row] = 0
			//			END IF
			// 		Add by Wcs 2012.07.06
			ll_count_jl = lds_syrc_new.Retrieve(ldt_begin,ldt_end,ls_ysbm,ls_jzks)
			IF ll_count_jl > 0 THEN
				dw_1.Object.syrc[ll_row] = lds_syrc_new.Object.syrc[ll_count_jl]
			ELSE
				dw_1.Object.syrc[ll_row] = 0
			END IF
			
			ll_find_cfk = lds_ypfy.Find("ysbm ='" + ls_ysbm + "'",1,ll_count_cfk)
			IF ll_find_cfk > 0 THEN
				dw_1.Object.ypjc[ll_row] = lds_ypfy.Object.je[ll_find_cfk] //(lds_ypfy.Object.je[ll_find_cfk]) / dw_1.Object.swjzrc[ll_row]
			ELSE
				dw_1.Object.ypjc[ll_row] = 0
			END IF
			
			ll_find_fy = lds_fyxx.Find("ysbm ='" + ls_ysbm + "'",1,ll_count_fy)
			IF ll_find_fy > 0 THEN
				dw_1.Object.rjfy[ll_row] = lds_fyxx.Object.zje[ll_find_fy]
			ELSE
				dw_1.Object.rjfy[ll_row] = 0
			END IF
			
			ll_count_jl = lds_wfxx.Retrieve(ls_ysbm,ldt_begin,ldt_end )
			
			IF ll_count_jl > 0 THEN
				dw_1.Object.wfsl[ll_row] = lds_wfxx.Object.wfsl[ll_count_jl]
			ELSE
				dw_1.Object.wfsl[ll_row] = 0
			END IF
			SELECT count(mz_yyxx.lxdh ) Into :ll_yyrs From mz_yyxx Where  (mz_yyxx.yysj >= :ldt_swsj And mz_yyxx.yysj <= :ldt_end) And (mz_yyxx.lxdh Is Not Null ) And (mz_yyxx.ksbm Is Not Null) And mz_yyxx.czbz <> -1 And ysbm = :ls_ysbm;
			SELECT sum(nvl(yyrs,0) + nvl(wwyyrs,0)) Into  :ll_kyrs From mz_pbxx Where (mz_pbxx.RQ = :ldt_day) And mz_pbxx.zxbz = '0' And mz_pbxx.ysbm = :ls_ysbm And bc = '01';
			dw_1.Object.kyrs[ll_row] = ll_kyrs
			dw_1.Object.yyrs[ll_row] = ll_yyrs
			//			dw_1.Object.xhrs[ll_row] = ll_xhrs
			//sycs
			ll_find = lds_sycs_sw.Find("ysbm ='" + ls_ysbm + "' and ksbm ='" + ls_jzks + "'",1,ll_count_sw)
			IF ll_find > 0 THEN
				dw_1.Object.sycs[ll_row] = lds_sycs_sw.Object.sycs[ll_find]
			END IF
			/*	//////////////// //
																											ll_find = lds_kjypxx.find("ysbm ='" + ls_ysbm + "'", 1,ll_count_kjyp)
																											if ll_find > 0 then
																												dw_1.Object.kjypfy[ll_row] = lds_kjypxx.object.kjypfy[ll_find]
																											end if
			*/
		ELSE
			dw_1.DeleteRow(ll_row)
			CONTINUE
			//			dw_1.Object.ghrc[ll_row] = 0
		END IF
		
		dw_1.Object.rjsc[ll_row] = gf_secondsafter(dw_1.Object.szsj[ll_row],dw_1.Object.mzsj[ll_row])/(60 * dw_1.Object.swjzrc[ll_row])
		
		
		IF ll_pbcount > 0 THEN
			ll_find = ids_pbxx.Find("bc ='01'", 1, ll_pbcount)
			IF ll_find > 0 THEN
				ls_bc = '上午'
				ls_ksbm = ids_pbxx.Object.ksbm[ll_find]
				ll_cs_find = dw_1.Find("ysbm ='" + ls_ysbm + "' and zzrq =datetime('" +String(ldt_rq)+ "') and bclb ='上午'",1,dw_1.RowCount())
				IF ll_cs_find > 0 THEN
					
					IF dw_1.Object.szsj[ll_row] > dw_1.Object.szsj[ll_cs_find] THEN
						dw_1.Object.szsj[ll_row] = dw_1.Object.szsj[ll_cs_find]
					ELSE
						dw_1.Object.szsj[ll_cs_find] = dw_1.Object.szsj[ll_row]
					END IF
					IF dw_1.Object.mzsj[ll_row] > dw_1.Object.mzsj[ll_cs_find] THEN
						dw_1.Object.mzsj[ll_cs_find] = dw_1.Object.mzsj[ll_row]
					ELSE
						dw_1.Object.mzsj[ll_row] = dw_1.Object.mzsj[ll_cs_find]
					END IF
				END IF
				//====================================================================
				//add by Wcs 2011.12.12
				//====================================================================
				
				IF IsNull(ldt_xtdr) Or ldt_xtdr > dw_1.Object.szsj[ll_row] THEN
					ldt_xtdr =   gf_relativedatetime('MINUTE',dw_1.Object.szsj[ll_row],15) //modify by Wcs 2012.01.18,改成15分钟
					IF Date(ldt_xtdr) <> Date(ldt_day) THEN
						ldt_xtdr = gf_relativedatetime('MINUTE',ldt_xtdr,-15)
					END IF
				END IF
				IF IsNull(ldt_xttc) Or ldt_xttc < dw_1.Object.mzsj[ll_row] THEN
					ldt_xttc =   gf_relativedatetime('MINUTE',dw_1.Object.mzsj[ll_row],-15)
					IF Date(ldt_xttc) <> Date(ldt_day) THEN
						ldt_xttc = gf_relativedatetime('MINUTE',ldt_xttc,15)
					END IF
				END IF
				
				IF Month(ldt_day) >= 7 And Month(ldt_day) < 9 THEN
					IF Not IsNull(ids_pbxx.Object.SBSJ[ll_find]) THEN
						IF Time(ids_pbxx.Object.SBSJ[ll_find]) > Time(ids_pbxx.Object.kssj_xl[ll_find]) THEN
							lt_sbsj = Time(ids_pbxx.Object.SBSJ[ll_find])
						ELSE
							lt_sbsj = Time(ids_pbxx.Object.kssj_xl[ll_find])
						END IF
					ELSE
						lt_sbsj = Time(ids_pbxx.Object.kssj_xl[ll_find])
					END IF
					IF Not IsNull(ids_pbxx.Object.xbsj[ll_find]) THEN
						IF Time(ids_pbxx.Object.xbsj[ll_find]) > Time(ids_pbxx.Object.zzsj_xl[ll_find]) THEN
							lt_xbsj = Time(ids_pbxx.Object.xbsj[ll_find])
						ELSE
							lt_xbsj = Time(ids_pbxx.Object.zzsj_xl[ll_find])
						END IF
					ELSE
						lt_xbsj = Time(ids_pbxx.Object.zzsj_xl[ll_find])
					END IF
					//					
					//					IF Time(ldt_xtdr) > RelativeTime(lt_sbsj,900) THEN //推后10分钟 2012.1.1 ,改为15分钟
					//						dw_1.Object.cdbz[ll_row] = '1'
					//		format="[time]" html.valueishtml="0"  name=zzsj_xl visible="1" edit.limit=0 edit.case=any edit.focusrectangle=no edit.autoselect=no edit.imemode=0  font.face="Arial" font.height="-12" font.weight="400"  font.family="2" font.pitch="2" font.charset="0" background.mode="1" background.color="536870912" )
column(band=detail id=3 alignment="0" tabsequence=32766 border="0" color="33554432" x="2962" y="12" height="76" width="238" format="[general]" html.valueishtml="0"  name=zzlb visible="1" edit.limit=0			ELSE 					//						dw_1.Object.cdbz[ll_row] = '0' 					//					END IF 					//					IF Time(ldt_xttc) < RelativeTime(lt_xbsj,-900) THEN //提前10分钟2012.1.1 ,改为15分钟
					//						dw_1.Object.ztbz[ll_row] = '1'
					//					ELSE
					//						dw_1.Object.ztbz[ll_row] = '0'
					//					END IF
					
				ELSE
					IF Not IsNull(ids_pbxx.Object.SBSJ[ll_find]) THEN
						IF Time(ids_pbxx.Object.SBSJ[ll_find]) > Time(ids_pbxx.Object.kssj[ll_find]) THEN
							lt_sbsj = Time(ids_pbxx.Object.SBSJ[ll_find])
						ELSE
							lt_sbsj = Time(ids_pbxx.Object.kssj[ll_find])
						END IF
					ELSE
						lt_sbsj = Time(ids_pbxx.Object.kssj[ll_find])
					END IF
					//					IF Not IsNull(ids_pbxx.Object.xbsj[ll_find]) THEN
					//						IF Time(ids_pbxx.Object.xbsj[ll_find]) > Time(ids_pbxx.Object.zzsj[ll_find]) THEN
					//							lt_xbsj = Time(ids_pbxx.Object.xbsj[ll_find])
					//						ELSE
					//							lt_xbsj = Time(ids_pbxx.Object.zzsj[ll_find])
					//						END IF
					//					ELSE
					lt_xbsj = Time(ids_pbxx.Object.zzsj[ll_find])
					//					END IF
					
				END IF
				IF ids_pbxx.Object.zzlb[ll_find] = dw_1.Object.jzlb[ll_row] THEN
					//注释 by hd,2017-04-20
//					IF Time(ldt_xtdr) > RelativeTime(lt_sbsj,900) THEN //推后10分钟 2012.1.1 ,改为15分钟
//						dw_1.Object.cdbz[ll_row] = '1'
//					ELSE
//						dw_1.Object.cdbz[ll_row] = '0'
//					END IF
					
					//============================================================================
					//add by hd,2017-04-20
					//判断是否迟到5分钟和30分钟
					IF Time(ldt_xtdr) > RelativeTime(lt_sbsj,359) and Time(ldt_xtdr) <= RelativeTime(lt_sbsj,1859) THEN //推后10分钟 2012.1.1 ,改为15分钟
						dw_1.Object.cdbz[ll_row] = '1'
						dw_1.Object.cdfive[ll_row] = '1'
						dw_1.Object.cdthirty[ll_row] = '0'
					ELSEIF Time(ldt_xtdr) > RelativeTime(lt_sbsj,1859) then
						dw_1.Object.cdbz[ll_row] = '1'
						dw_1.Object.cdfive[ll_row] = '1'
						dw_1.Object.cdthirty[ll_row] = '1'
					Else
						dw_1.Object.cdbz[ll_row] = '0'
						dw_1.Object.cdfive[ll_row] = '0'
						dw_1.Object.cdthirty[ll_row] = '0'
					END IF
					//============================================================================
					
					IF Time(ldt_xttc) < RelativeTime(lt_xbsj,-900) THEN //提前10分钟2012.1.1 ,改为15分钟
						dw_1.Object.ztbz[ll_row] = '1'
					ELSE
						dw_1.Object.ztbz[ll_row] = '0'
					END IF
				END IF
				//====================================================================			
				IF ids_pbxx.Object.zzlb[ll_find] = '2' THEN //add by Wcs 2011.11.02 ,只有平诊才计算超时人数
					ll_find_ks = lds_mzks_kz.Find("ksbm ='" + ls_ksbm + "'",1,ll_count_ks)
					IF ll_find_ks > 0 THEN
						IF Month(ldt_day) >= 7 And Month(ldt_day) < 9 THEN
							ll_csrs_bz = lds_mzks_kz.Object.cssw_xl[ll_find_ks]
						ELSE
							ll_csrs_bz = lds_mzks_kz.Object.cssw_dl[ll_find_ks]
						END IF
						IF IsNull(ll_csrs_bz) THEN ll_csrs_bz = 0
						//					IF Time(ldt_xtdr) >= RelativeTime(Time(dw_1.Object.szsj[ll_row]),-1800) THEN
						IF dw_1.Object.szsj[ll_row] < ldt_swsj THEN
							ldt_cssj = gf_relativedatetime('MINUTE',ldt_swsj,-ldc_sw)
						ELSE
							ldt_cssj = gf_relativedatetime('MINUTE',dw_1.Object.szsj[ll_row],-ldc_sw)
						END IF
						IF dw_1.Object.mzsj[ll_row] > ldt_cssj And ll_csrs_bz > 0 THEN //And Time(dw_1.Object.mzsj[ll_row]) > lt_xbsj THEN
							If (dw_1.Object.swjzrc[ll_row] - dw_1.Object.mfjz[ll_row] ) > ll_csrs_bz THEN
								lds_ghxx_cs.SetFilter('')
								lds_ghxx_cs.Filter()
								lds_ghxx_cs.Retrieve(ldt_cssj,ldt_end,ls_ysbm,ls_jzlb,ls_jzks)
								lds_ghxx_cs.SetFilter("jsbz ='1' and not (thbz ='3' and ghlx =80)")
								lds_ghxx_cs.Filter( )
								//ll_csrs = lds_ghxx_cs.rowcount() - ll_csrs_bz
								ll_csrs_gh = lds_ghxx_cs.RowCount()
								IF ll_csrs_gh > (dw_1.Object.swjzrc[ll_row] - dw_1.Object.mfjz[ll_row] - ll_csrs_bz) THEN
									ll_csrs = dw_1.Object.swjzrc[ll_row] - dw_1.Object.mfjz[ll_row] - ll_csrs_bz
								ELSE
									ll_csrs = ll_csrs_gh
								END IF
								
								IF ll_csrs < 0 THEN ll_csrs = 0
								dw_1.Object.csrc[ll_row] = ll_csrs
							ELSE
								dw_1.Object.csrc[ll_row] = 0
							END IF
						ELSE
							dw_1.Object.csrc[ll_row] = 0
						END IF
					ELSE
						dw_1.Object.csrc[ll_row] = 0
					END IF
					//					ELSE
					//						dw_1.Object.csrc[ll_row] = 0
					//					END IF
				ELSE
					dw_1.Object.csrc[ll_row] = 0
				END IF
				// ADD by Wcs  2011.12.31 
				dw_1.Object.pbks[ll_row] = ls_ksbm
			END IF
		ELSE
			ls_bc = '其他上'
			dw_1.Object.csrc[ll_row] = 0
		END IF
		IF ls_bc = '' THEN ls_bc = '其他上'
		dw_1.Object.bclb[ll_row] = ls_bc
		dw_1.Object.SBSJ[ll_row] = ldt_xtdr
		dw_1.Object.xbsj[ll_row] = ldt_xttc
		
		IF IsNull(dw_1.Object.csrc[ll_row]) THEN dw_1.Object.csrc[ll_row] = 0
	NEXT
	
	////////////// //下午
	dw_2.Retrieve(ldt_begin1,ldt_end1)
	ll_count_cfk = lds_ypfy.Retrieve(ldt_begin1,ldt_end1)
	ll_count_fy = lds_fyxx.Retrieve(ldt_begin1,ldt_end1)
	// add by Wcs 抗菌药物费用 下午
	//	ll_count_kjyp = lds_kjypxx.retrieve(ldt_begin1,ldt_end1)
	ll_count = dw_2.RowCount( )
	FOR ll_i = 1 To ll_count
		ls_bc = ''
		ls_ysbm = dw_2.Object.ysbm[ll_i]
		//		ls_yq = dw_2.Object.yybz[ll_i]
		ls_jzks = dw_2.Object.jzks[ll_i]
		ls_jzlb = dw_2.Object.jzlb[ll_i]
		ll_zlxz = dw_2.Object.zlxz[ll_i]
		ll_pbcount = ids_pbxx.Retrieve(ldt_rq,ls_ysbm)
		//		IF ls_ysbm = '192022' THEN
		//			DebugBreak()
		//		END IF
		//			ll_pbcount = ids_pbxx.Retrieve(ldt_rq,ls_ysbm)
		//			if ll_pbcount <= 0 then
		//				ll_bc_find = dw_1.find("bclb ='其他上'",1,dw_1.rowcount())
		//				if ll_bc_find > 0 then
		//					ll_row = ll_bc_find
		//				else
		//					ll_row = dw_1.InsertRow(0)
		//				end if
		//			else
		//				
		//			end if
		ll_row = dw_1.InsertRow(0)
		dw_1.Object.zzrq[ll_row] = ldt_rq //Date(dw_2.Object.jzsj[ll_i])
		
		dw_1.Object.jzks[ll_row] = ls_jzks
		dw_1.Object.ysbm[ll_row] = ls_ysbm
		dw_1.Object.zlxz[ll_row] = ll_zlxz
		ldt_begin2 = gf_relativedatetime('MINUTE',ldt_begin1,30)
		SELECT Min(jlsj) Into :ldt_xtdr From ss_xtrz Where xtsb = 10 And czgh = :ls_ysbm And rzlx = 'XTDR' And jlsj >= :ldt_xwsj And jlsj < :ldt_end1;
		SELECT Max(jlsj) Into :ldt_xttc From ss_xtrz Where xtsb = 10 And czgh = :ls_ysbm And rzlx = 'XTTC' And jlsj >= :ldt_xwsj And jlsj < :ldt_end1;
		
		dw_1.Object.jzlb[ll_row] = ls_jzlb
		lds_ghxx.SetFilter("")
		lds_ghxx.Filter( )
		ll_count_jl = lds_ghxx.Retrieve(ldt_begin1,ldt_end1,ls_ysbm,ls_jzlb,ls_jzks,ll_zlxz)
		ll_count_et = lds_ghxx_et.Retrieve(ldt_begin1,ldt_end1,ls_ysbm,ls_jzlb,ls_jzks,ll_zlxz)
		ll_count_gh = lds_gh.Retrieve(ldt_begin1,ldt_end1,ls_ysbm) //ghrc
		IF ll_count_gh > 0 THEN
			dw_1.Object.ghrc[ll_row] = lds_gh.Object.ghrc[1]
		ELSE
			dw_1.Object.ghrc[ll_row] = 0
		END IF
		//限号人数，固定班挂号人数
		ll_find = lds_pbxx.Find("ysbm ='" + ls_ysbm + "' and ksbm ='" + ls_jzks + "' and bc ='02' and zzlb ='" + ls_jzlb + "'", 1, ll_count_xh)
		IF ll_find > 0 THEN
			dw_1.Object.xhrs[ll_row] = lds_pbxx.Object.xhrs[ll_find]
			IF Date(ldt_rq) = Date(lds_pbxx.Object.czsj[ll_find]) THEN
				dw_1.Object.ghrcgd[ll_row] = 0
			ELSE
				ll_find_jzlb = lds_ghxx_jzlbxw.Find("ysbm='" + ls_ysbm+ "' and jzks ='" + ls_jzks + "' and hzlb ='" + ls_jzlb + "'", 1, ll_jzlb_xw)
				IF ll_find_jzlb > 0 THEN
					dw_1.Object.ghrcgd[ll_row] = lds_ghxx_jzlbxw.Object.ghrc[ll_find_jzlb]
				ELSE
					dw_1.Object.ghrcgd[ll_row] = 0
				END IF
			END IF
		END IF
		
		IF ll_count_jl > 0 THEN
			dw_1.Object.xwjzrc[ll_row] = lds_ghxx.Object.compute_jzrs[1]
			
			dw_1.Object.szsj[ll_row] = lds_ghxx.Object.compute_minjzsj[1]
			dw_1.Object.mzsj[ll_row] = lds_ghxx.Object.compute_maxjzsj[1]
			
			lds_ghxx.SetFilter("jsbz ='1' and not (thbz ='3' and ghlx =80)")
			lds_ghxx.Filter( )
			ll_ghrs = lds_ghxx.RowCount( )
			
			IF ll_ghrs > 0 THEN
				dw_1.Object.gzl[ll_row] = lds_ghxx.Object.compute_gzl[1]

			ELSE
				dw_1.Object.gzl[ll_row] = 0
				//				dw_1.Object.ghrc[ll_row] = 0
			END IF
			
			
			//			lds_ghxx.SetFilter("")
			//			lds_ghxx.Filter( )
			lds_ghxx.SetFilter("thbz ='3' and ghlx =80")

			//			IF lds_ghxx.RowCount( ) > 0 THEN
			//				ll_mfjz = lds_ghxx.Object.compute_jzrs[1]
			//				ldc_mfjz_gzl = lds_ghxx.Object.compute_gzl[1]
			//				dw_1.Object.gzl[ll_row] = dw_1.Object.gzl[ll_row] - ldc_mfjz_gzl

			//			END IF
			dw_1.Object.mfjz[ll_row] = ll_mfjz
			//			lds_ghxx.SetFilter("")

			//			ll_count_jl = lds_syxx.Retrieve(ldt_begin1,ldt_end1,ls_ysbm,ls_jzlb,ls_jzks)  // 注释时间 2012.07.06

			//				dw_1.Object.syrc[ll_row] = lds_syxx.Object.syrc[ll_count_jl]

			//			END IF

			dw_1.Object.etgzl[ll_row] = wf_ghxx_et(lds_ghxx_et)
			
			// 		Add by Wcs 2012.07.06
			ll_count_jl = lds_syrc_new.Retrieve(ldt_begin1,ldt_end1,ls_ysbm,ls_jzks)
			IF ll_count_jl > 0 THEN
				dw_1.Object.syrc[ll_row] = lds_syrc_new.Object.syrc[ll_count_jl]
			ELSE
				dw_1.Object.syrc[ll_row] = 0
			END IF
			
			ll_find_cfk = lds_ypfy.Find("ysbm ='" + ls_ysbm + "'",1,ll_count_cfk)
			IF ll_find_cfk > 0 THEN
				dw_1.Object.ypjc[ll_row] = lds_ypfy.Object.je[ll_find_cfk] //(lds_ypfy.Object.je[ll_find_cfk]) / dw_1.Object.xwjzrc[ll_row]
			ELSE
				dw_1.Object.ypjc[ll_row] = 0
			END IF
			
			ll_find_fy = lds_fyxx.Find("ysbm ='" + ls_ysbm + "'",1,ll_count_fy)
			IF ll_find_fy > 0 THEN
				dw_1.Object.rjfy[ll_row] = lds_fyxx.Object.zje[ll_find_fy]
			ELSE
				dw_1.Object.rjfy[ll_row] = 0
			END IF
			
			ll_count_jl = lds_wfxx.Retrieve(ls_ysbm,ldt_begin1,ldt_end1 )
			IF ll_count_jl > 0 THEN
				dw_1.Object.wfsl[ll_row] = lds_wfxx.Object.wfsl[ll_count_jl]
			ELSE
				dw_1.Object.wfsl[ll_row] = 0
			END IF
			SELECT count(mz_yyxx.lxdh ) Into :ll_yyrs From mz_yyxx Where  (mz_yyxx.yysj >= :ldt_xwsj And mz_yyxx.yysj <= :ldt_end1) And (mz_yyxx.lxdh Is Not Null ) And (mz_yyxx.ksbm Is Not Null) And mz_yyxx.czbz <> -1 And ysbm = :ls_ysbm;
			SELECT sum(nvl(yyrs,0) + nvl(wwyyrs,0)) Into  :ll_kyrs From mz_pbxx Where (mz_pbxx.RQ = :ldt_day) And mz_pbxx.zxbz = '0' And mz_pbxx.ysbm = :ls_ysbm And bc = '02';
			dw_1.Object.kyrs[ll_row] = ll_kyrs
			dw_1.Object.yyrs[ll_row] = ll_yyrs
			//			dw_1.Object.xhrs[ll_row] = ll_xhrs
			//sycs
			ll_find = lds_sycs_xw.Find("ysbm ='" + ls_ysbm + "' and ksbm ='" + ls_jzks + "'",1,ll_count_xw)
			IF ll_find > 0 THEN
				dw_1.Object.sycs[ll_row] = lds_sycs_xw.Object.sycs[ll_find]
			END IF
			/*//////////////// //
																													ll_find = lds_kjypxx.find("ysbm ='" + ls_ysbm + "'", 1,ll_count_kjyp)
																													if ll_find > 0 then
																														dw_1.Object.kjypfy[ll_row] = lds_kjypxx.object.kjypfy[ll_find]
			end if */
		ELSE
			dw_1.DeleteRow(ll_row)
			CONTINUE
			//			dw_1.Object.ghrc[ll_row] = 0
		END IF
		
		dw_1.Object.rjsc[ll_row] = gf_secondsafter(dw_1.Object.szsj[ll_row],dw_1.Object.mzsj[ll_row])/(60 * dw_1.Object.xwjzrc[ll_row])
		
		
		IF ll_pbcount > 0 THEN
			ll_find = ids_pbxx.Find("bc ='02'", 1, ll_pbcount)
			
			IF ll_find > 0 THEN
				ls_ksbm = ids_pbxx.Object.ksbm[ll_find]
				ls_bc = '下午'
				ll_cs_find = dw_1.Find("ysbm ='" + ls_ysbm + "' and zzrq =datetime('" +String(ldt_rq)+ "') and bclb ='下午'",1,dw_1.RowCount())
				IF ll_cs_find > 0 THEN
					IF dw_1.Object.szsj[ll_row] > dw_1.Object.szsj[ll_cs_find] THEN
						dw_1.Object.szsj[ll_row] = dw_1.Object.szsj[ll_cs_find]
					ELSE
						dw_1.Object.szsj[ll_cs_find] = dw_1.Object.szsj[ll_row]
					END IF
					IF dw_1.Object.mzsj[ll_row] > dw_1.Object.mzsj[ll_cs_find] THEN
						dw_1.Object.mzsj[ll_cs_find] = dw_1.Object.mzsj[ll_row]
					ELSE
						dw_1.Object.mzsj[ll_row] = dw_1.Object.mzsj[ll_cs_find]
					END IF
				END IF
				//====================================================================
				//add by Wcs 2011.12.12
				//====================================================================
				
				IF IsNull(ldt_xtdr) Or ldt_xtdr > dw_1.Object.szsj[ll_row] THEN
					ldt_xtdr =   gf_relativedatetime('MINUTE',dw_1.Object.szsj[ll_row],15)
					IF Date(ldt_xtdr) <> Date(ldt_day) THEN
						ldt_xtdr = gf_relativedatetime('MINUTE',ldt_xtdr,-15)
					END IF
				END IF
				IF IsNull(ldt_xttc) Or ldt_xttc < dw_1.Object.mzsj[ll_row] THEN
					ldt_xttc =   gf_relativedatetime('MINUTE',dw_1.Object.mzsj[ll_row],-15)
					IF Date(ldt_xttc) <> Date(ldt_day) THEN
						ldt_xttc = gf_relativedatetime('MINUTE',ldt_xttc,15)
					END IF
				END IF
				IF Month(ldt_day) >= 7 And Month(ldt_day) < 9 THEN
					IF Not IsNull(ids_pbxx.Object.SBSJ[ll_find]) THEN
						IF Time(ids_pbxx.Object.SBSJ[ll_find]) > Time(ids_pbxx.Object.kssj_xl[ll_find]) THEN
							lt_sbsj = Time(ids_pbxx.Object.SBSJ[ll_find])
						ELSE
							lt_sbsj = Time(ids_pbxx.Object.kssj_xl[ll_find])
						END IF
					ELSE
						lt_sbsj = Time(ids_pbxx.Object.kssj_xl[ll_find])
					END IF
					IF Not IsNull(ids_pbxx.Object.xbsj[ll_find]) THEN
						IF Time(ids_pbxx.Object.xbsj[ll_find]) > Time(ids_pbxx.Object.zzsj_xl[ll_find]) THEN
							lt_xbsj = Time(ids_pbxx.Object.xbsj[ll_find])
						ELSE
							lt_xbsj = Time(ids_pbxx.Object.zzsj_xl[ll_find])
						END IF
					ELSE
						lt_xbsj = Time(ids_pbxx.Object.zzsj_xl[ll_find])
					END IF
					
					
				ELSE
					IF Not IsNull(ids_pbxx.Object.SBSJ[ll_find]) THEN
						IF Time(ids_pbxx.Object.SBSJ[ll_find]) > Time(ids_pbxx.Object.kssj[ll_find]) THEN
							lt_sbsj = Time(ids_pbxx.Object.SBSJ[ll_find])
						ELSE
							lt_sbsj = Time(ids_pbxx.Object.kssj[ll_find])
						END IF
					ELSE
						lt_sbsj = Time(ids_pbxx.Object.kssj[ll_find])
					END IF

					//						ELSE

					//						END IF
					//					ELSE
					lt_xbsj = Time(ids_pbxx.Object.zzsj[ll_find])
					//					END IF
					
				END IF

_row] = '0'
					END IF
					//============================================================================
					IF Time(ldt_xttc) < RelativeTime(lt_xbsj,-900) THEN //提前10分钟2012.1.1 ,改为15分钟
						dw_1.Object.ztbz[ll_row] = '1'
					ELSE
						dw_1.Object.ztbz[ll_row] = '0'
					END IF
				END IF
				//====================================================================	
				
				IF ids_pbxx.Object.zzlb[ll_find] = '2' THEN //add by Wcs 2011.11.02 ,只有平诊才计算超时人数
					//					IF Time(ldt_xtdr) >= RelativeTime(Time(dw_1.Object.szsj[ll_row]),-1800) THEN
					ll_find_ks = lds_mzks_kz.Find("ksbm ='" + ls_ksbm + "'",1,ll_count_ks)
					IF ll_find_ks > 0 THEN
						IF Month(ldt_day) >= 7 And Month(ldt_day) < 9 THEN
							ll_csrs_bz = lds_mzks_kz.Object.csxw_xl[ll_find_ks]
						ELSE
							ll_csrs_bz = lds_mzks_kz.Object.csxw_dl[ll_find_ks]
						END IF
						IF IsNull(ll_csrs_bz) THEN ll_csrs_bz = 0
						ldt_cssj = gf_relativedatetime('MINUTE',dw_1.Object.szsj[ll_row],-ldc_xw)
						IF dw_1.Object.mzsj[ll_row] > ldt_cssj And ll_csrs_bz > 0  THEN //And  Time(dw_1.Object.mzsj[ll_row]) > lt_xbsj THEN
							If (dw_1.Object.xwjzrc[ll_row] - dw_1.Object.mfjz[ll_row] ) > ll_csrs_bz THEN
								lds_ghxx_cs.SetFilter('')
								lds_ghxx_cs.Filter()
								lds_ghxx_cs.Retrieve(ldt_cssj,ldt_end1,ls_ysbm,ls_jzlb,ls_jzks)
								lds_ghxx_cs.SetFilter("jsbz ='1' and not (thbz ='3' and ghlx =80)")
								lds_ghxx_cs.Filter( )
								//								ll_csrs = lds_ghxx_cs.rowcount() - ll_csrs_bz
								ll_csrs_gh = lds_ghxx_cs.RowCount()
								IF ll_csrs_gh > (dw_1.Object.xwjzrc[ll_row] - dw_1.Object.mfjz[ll_row] - ll_csrs_bz) THEN
									ll_csrs = dw_1.Object.xwjzrc[ll_row] - dw_1.Object.mfjz[ll_row] - ll_csrs_bz
								ELSE
									ll_csrs = ll_csrs_gh
								END IF
								IF ll_csrs < 0 THEN ll_csrs = 0
								dw_1.Object.csrc[ll_row] = ll_csrs
							ELSE
								dw_1.Object.csrc[ll_row] = 0
							END IF
						ELSE
							dw_1.Object.csrc[ll_row] = 0
						END IF
					ELSE
						dw_1.Object.csrc[ll_row] = 0
					END IF
				ELSE
					dw_1.Object.csrc[ll_row] = 0
				END IF
				
				// ADD by Wcs  2011.12.31 
				dw_1.Object.pbks[ll_row] = ls_ksbm //ids_pbxx.Object.ksbm[ll_find]
			END IF
			
		ELSE
			ls_bc = '其他下'
			dw_1.Object.csrc[ll_row] = 0
		END IF
		IF ls_bc = '' THEN ls_bc = '其他下'
		dw_1.Object.bclb[ll_row] = ls_bc
		dw_1.Object.SBSJ[ll_row] = ldt_xtdr
		dw_1.Object.xbsj[ll_row] = ldt_xttc
	NEXT
	
	ldt_day = RelativeDate(Date(ldt_day),1)
NEXT

	gf_commit_transaction(sqlca)

	gf_applog('统计成功！')
ELSE
	gf_rollback_transaction(sqlca)
	gf_applog('统计失败：' + sqlca.SQLErrText)
END IF

gf_begin_transaction(sqlca)

ldt_day = Date(Left(em_1.Text,10))
ldt_day = RelativeDate(ldt_day,-15)
//DateTime ldt_myd_max
date ld_myd_b,ld_myd_e
string ls_hfsj,ls_hfsj_y
select csz into :ls_hfsj_y from gy_xtcs where xtsb =92 and csmc ='HFSJ-GZL';  //HFSJ-GZL
select csz into :ls_hfsj from gy_xtcs where xtsb =92 and csmc ='HFSJ';

	ld_myd_b =date(mid(ls_hfsj_y,1,10))
	ld_myd_e =date(mid(ls_hfsj,1,10))
	if daysafter(ld_myd_b,ld_myd_e) > 15 then
	else
		ld_myd_b = ldt_day//Date(Left(em_1.Text,10))
	end if
	DO WHILE Date(ld_myd_b) <= ld_myd_e
		
		ldt_begin = DateTime(Date(ld_myd_b),Time("00:00:00"))
		ldt_end = DateTime(Date(ld_myd_b),Time("23:59:59"))
		lds_dxmyd.Reset( )
		//dw_3.dbcancel( )
		dw_3.Reset( )
		ll_count_myd = lds_dxmyd.Retrieve('YS',0,ldt_begin,ldt_end )
		//	FOR ll_r = 1 To lds_dxmyd.RowCount()
		//		//		ldc_myd = lds_dxmyd.Object.jhpl[ll_r]
		//		ll_hprs = lds_dxmyd.Object.hps[ll_r]
		//		ll_cprs = lds_dxmyd.Object.cps[ll_r]
		//		ll_cgrs = lds_dxmyd.Object.cgrc[ll_r]
		//		ls_ysbm = lds_dxmyd.Object.zsm[ll_r]
		//		UPDATE mz_gzltj Set hprs = :ll_hprs,cprs = :ll_cprs,cgrs = :ll_cgrs Where zzrq = :ldt_day And ysbm = :ls_ysbm Using sqlca;
		//		if sqlca.sqlcode <> 0 then goto error
		//	NEXT
		ll_count = dw_3.Retrieve(Date(ld_myd_b),Date(ld_myd_b))
		FOR ll_i = 1 To ll_count
			ls_ysbm = dw_3.Object.ysbm[ll_i]
			ll_find = lds_dxmyd.Find( "zsm ='" + ls_ysbm + "'",1, ll_count_myd)
			IF ll_find > 0 THEN
				dw_3.Object.cgrs[ll_i] = lds_dxmyd.Object.cgrc[ll_find]
				dw_3.Object.cprs[ll_i] = lds_dxmyd.Object.cps[ll_find]
				dw_3.Object.hprs[ll_i] = lds_dxmyd.Object.hps[ll_find]
			ELSE
				dw_3.Object.cgrs[ll_i] = 0
				dw_3.Object.cprs[ll_i] = 0
				dw_3.Object.hprs[ll_i] = 0
			END IF
		NEXT
		FOR ll_i = 1 To ll_count
			IF dw_3.Object.cgrs[ll_i] > 0 Or dw_3.Object.cprs[ll_i] > 0 Or dw_3.Object.hprs[ll_i] > 0 THEN
				IF ldt_rq = Date(dw_3.Object.zzrq[ll_i]) And ls_ysbm = dw_3.Object.ysbm[ll_i]  THEN
					dw_3.Object.cgrs[ll_i] = 0
					dw_3.Object.cprs[ll_i] = 0
					dw_3.Object.hprs[ll_i] = 0
				ELSE
					ls_ysbm = dw_3.Object.ysbm[ll_i]
					ls_bc = dw_3.Object.bclb[ll_i]
					ldt_rq = Date(dw_3.Object.zzrq[ll_i])
				END IF
			ELSE
				dw_3.Object.cgrs[ll_i] = 0
				dw_3.Object.cprs[ll_i] = 0
				dw_3.Object.hprs[ll_i] = 0
			END IF
		NEXT
		
		IF dw_3.Update( ) = 1 THEN
			update gy_xtcs set csz=:ls_hfsj where xtsb =92 and csmc ='HFSJ-GZL';
			if sqlca.sqlcode = 0 then
				gf_commit_transaction(sqlca)
				gf_applog('满意度成功！'+string(ld_myd_b))
			else
				ls_error='满意度回复时间更新失败'
				goto goerror
			end if
			//	Close(Parent)
			//	MessageBox('提示','统计成功！' +String(Left(em_1.Text,10))+ ',' +String(Left(em_2.Text,10)) )
		ELSE
			goerror:
			ls_error = ls_error + sqlca.SQLErrText
			gf_rollback_transaction(sqlca)
			gf_applog('满意度统计失败：' +ls_error )
		END IF
		ld_myd_b =RelativeDate(Date(ld_myd_b),1)
	LOOP

Destroy lds_ghxx
destroy lds_ghxx_et
Destroy lds_syxx
Destroy lds_ypfy
Destroy lds_gh
Destroy lds_dxmyd
Destroy lds_sycs_sw
Destroy lds_sycs_xw
Destroy lds_syrc_new
Destroy lds_kjypxx
Destroy lds_mzks_kz
Destroy lds_ghxx_cs
gf_applog('程序退出！')

	Close(Parent)

//IF cbx_1.Checked = True THEN
//	ll_daynum = DayNumber(Date(Now()))
//	IF Now() <= Time('03:00') And ll_daynum > 1 And ll_daynum < 7 THEN
//		shutdown(1)
//	END IF
//END IF



















end event

global w_dx w_dx

on w_dx.create
this.cb_5=create cb_5
this.cb_4=create cb_4
this.cb_3=create cb_3

this.cb_2=create cb_2

this.Control[]={this.cb_5,&
this.cb_4,&
this.cb_3,&
this.dw_1,&
this.cb_2,&
this.cb_1,&
this.dw_import}
end on

destroy(this.cb_4)
destroy(this.cb_3)
destroy(this.dw_1)
destroy(this.cb_2)
destroy(this.cb_1)
destroy(this.dw_import)
end on

event clicked;long ll_count ,ll_i,ll_find
string ls_ksmc ,ls_ksbm,ls_fybm,ls_ksbm_old,ls_fybm_old,ls_fydj,ls_temp
dec{2} ldc_fydj,ldc_fydj_old
dw_import.setsort('c1 a,c3 a,c6 a')
dw_import.sort( )
for ll_i =1 to dw_import.rowcount( )
	ls_ksbm = dw_import.object.c1[ll_i]
	dw_import.object.c1[ll_i] = right("0000"+ dw_import.object.c1[ll_i],4)

next
//dw_import.accepttext( )
dw_import.saveas( )
end event

type cb_4 from commandbutton within w_dx
integer x = 2656

end type

event clicked;long ll_count ,ll_i,ll_find
string ls_ksmc ,ls_ksbm,ls_fybm,ls_ksbm_old,ls_fybm_old,ls_fydj,ls_temp
dec{2} ldc_fydj,ldc_fydj_old
dw_import.setsort('c1 a,c3 a,c6 a')
dw_import.sort( )
for ll_i =1 to dw_import.rowcount( )
	ls_ksbm = dw_import.object.c1[ll_i]
	ls_fybm =  dw_import.object.c3[ll_i]
	ls_fydj = dw_import.object.c6[ll_i]
	ldc_fydj = dec( dw_import.object.c6[ll_i])
//	find:
//	ll_find = dw_import.find("c1 ='" + ls_ksbm + "' and c3 ='" + ls_fybm + "' and c6 = '" + ls_fydj +"'",ll_i +1,dw_import.rowcount( ))
//	if ll_find > 0 then
//		dw_import.object.c7[ll_i] = dw_import.object.c7[ll_i] + dw_import.object.c7[ll_find]
//		dw_import.deleterow(ll_find)
//		do while ll_find >0
//			goto find
//		loop 
//	end if
	if ls_ksbm = ls_ksbm_old and ls_fybm = ls_fybm_old  then
		if ldc_fydj <> ldc_fydj_old then
			ls_temp = right(ls_fybm,2)
			ls_temp = string(right('00'+string((long(ls_temp) + 1)),2))
			dw_import.object.c3[ll_i] = mid(ls_fybm,1,len(ls_fybm) -2) +ls_temp
		end if
	else
		ls_ksbm_old = ls_ksbm 
		ls_fybm_old = ls_fybm
	end if
next
//dw_import.accepttext( )
dw_import.saveas( )
end event

dw_import.sort( )
for ll_i =1 to dw_import.rowcount( )
	ls_ksbm = dw_import.object.c1[ll_i]
	ls_fybm =  dw_import.object.c3[ll_i]
	ls_fydj = dw_import.object.c6[ll_i]
	ldc_fydj = dec( dw_import.object.c6[ll_i])
	if ls_ksbm ='0001' and ls_fybm ='11020000101' then debugbreak()
	find:
	ll_find = dw_import.find("c1 ='" + ls_ksbm + "' and c3 ='" + ls_fybm + "' and c6 = '" + ls_fydj +"'",ll_i +1,dw_import.rowcount( ))
	if ll_find > 0 then
		dw_import.object.c7[ll_i] = string(round(dec(dw_import.object.c7[ll_i]) + dec(dw_import.object.c7[ll_find]),2))
		dw_import.deleterow(ll_find)
		do while ll_find >0
			goto find
		loop 
	end if
//	if ls_ksbm = ls_ksbm_old and ls_fybm = ls_fybm_old and ldc_fytext)
blob{1} lb_0

	ls_ksmc =dw_1.object.mc[ll_i]
	ll_find = dw_import.find("c1 ='" + ls_ksmc + "' and c2='上午'", 1, ll_count)
	if ll_find >0 then
		dw_1.object.cssw_dl[ll_i] = long(dw_import.object.c3[ll_find])
	else
		dw_1.object.cssw_dl[ll_i] = 0
	end if
	ll_find = dw_import.find("c1 ='" + ls_ksmc + "' and c2='下午'", 1, ll_count)
	if ll_find >0 then
		dw_1.object.csxw_dl[ll_i] = long(dw_import.object.c3[ll_find])
	else
		dw_1.object.csxw_dl[ll_i] = 0
	end if
next

	gf_commit_transaction(sqlca)

forward
global type w_ghxx from window
end type
type em_1 from editmask within w_ghxx
end type
type em_2 from editmask within w_ghxx
end type
type cb_1 from commandbutton within w_ghxx
end type
end forward

global type w_ghxx from window
integer width = 4160
integer height = 1924
boolean titlebar = true
string title = "Untitled"
boolean controlmenu = true
boolean minbox = true
boolean maxbox = true
boolean resizable = true
long backcolor = 67108864
string icon = "AppIcon!"
boolean center = true
em_1 em_1
em_2 em_2
cb_1 cb_1
end type
global w_ghxx w_ghxx

on w_ghxx.create
this.em_1=create em_1
this.em_2=create em_2
this.cb_1=create cb_1
this.Control[]={this.em_1,&
this.em_2,&
this.cb_1}
end on

on w_ghxx.destroy
destroy(this.em_1)
destroy(this.em_2)
destroy(this.cb_1)
end on

end type

event clicked;datetime ld_begin ,ld_end
long ll_count,ll_i,ll_js,ll_c
dec{0} ldc_ghlsh 
string ls_brbm
ld_begin = DateTime(Date(Left(em_1.Text,10)),Time('00:00:00'))
ld_end = DateTime(Date(Left(em_2.Text,10)),Time('23:59:59'))
datastore lds_ghxx
lds_ghxx = create datastore
lds_ghxx.dataobject ='d_ghxx_3'
lds_ghxx.settransobject(sqlca)
ll_count = lds_ghxx.retrieve(ld_begin ,ld_end)
for ll_i = 1 to ll_count 
	ldc_ghlsh = lds_ghxx.object.ghlsh[ll_i]
	ls_brbm = lds_ghxx.object.brbm[ll_i]
	select count(yjlsh) into :ll_c from mz_yjk1 where ghlsh = :ldc_ghlsh and brbm =:ls_brbm and kpsj is null and kdrq >= :ld_begin;
	if ll_c > 0 then
		select count(cflsh) into :ll_c from mz_cfk where ghlsh = :ldc_ghlsh and brbm =:ls_brbm ;//and kpsj is null and cfsj >= :ld_begin;
		
		if ll_c > 0 then
			select count(cflsh) into :ll_c from mz_cfk where ghlsh = :ldc_ghlsh and fontcharset

event clicked;datetime ldt_b,ldt_e
date ldt_day
long ll_day,ll_j
ll_day = DaysAfter(Date(Left(em_1.Text,10)),Date(Left(em_2.Text,10)))
IF ll_day < 0 THEN ll_day = 0
ldt_day = Date(Left(em_1.Text,10))

FOR ll_j = 1 To ll_day + 1
	ldt_b = datetime(ldt_day,time('00:00:00'))
	ldt_e = datetime(ldt_day,time('23:59:59'))
	dw_detail.reset( )
	if dw_detail.retrieve(ldt_b,ldt_e) > 0 then
		dw_main.insertrow(1)
		dw_main.object.swl[1] = dw_detail.object.swl[1]
		dw_main.object.jzsj[1] = dw_detail.object.jzsj[1]
	end if
	if dw_2.retrieve(ldt_b,ldt_e) > 0 then
		dw_main.insertrow(1)
		dw_main.object.swl[1] = dw_detail.object.swl[1]
		dw_main.object.jzsj[1] = dw_detail.object.jzsj[1]
	end if
	ldt_day = RelativeDate(Date(ldt_day),1)
next
end event

type cb_6 from commandbutton within w_gzltj
integer x = 1792
integer y = 352
integer width = 334
integer height = 84
integer taborder = 30
integer textsize = -10
integer weight = 400
fontcharset fontcharset = gb2312charset!
fontpitch fontpitch = fixed!
fontfamily fontfamily = modern!
string facename = "新宋藾AT* "
boolean enabled = false
string text = "迟到早退"
end type

event clicked;Date ldt_begin,ldt_end,ld_zzrq
String ls_bc,ls_ysbm,ls_zzlb,ls_bclb
DateTime ldt_sbsj,ldt_xbsj,ldt_szsj,ldt_mzsj
Long ll_i ,ll_row,ll_count
ldt_begin = Date(Left(em_1.Text,10))
ldt_end = Date(Left(em_2.Text,10))
dw_3.SetTransObject(sqlca)
dw_3.Retrieve(ldt_begin,ldt_end)
//dw_3.setfilter("ysbm ='203020'")
//dw_3.filter( )
ll_count = dw_3.rowcount( )
IF ll_count > 0 THEN
	FOR ll_i = 1 To ll_count
		ls_bclb = dw_3.Object.bclb[ll_i]
		IF ls_bclb = '上午' Or ls_bclb = '下午' THEN
			ldt_sbsj = dw_3.Object.sbsj[ll_i]
			ldt_xbsj = dw_3.Object.xbsj[ll_i]
			ldt_szsj = dw_3.Object.szsj[ll_i]
			ldt_mzsj = dw_3.Object.mzsj[ll_i]
			IF ls_bclb = '上午' THEN
				IF ldt_szsj < ldt_sbsj THEN
					ldt_sbsj = gf_relativedatetime('MINUTE',ldt_szsj,2)
					IF dw_3.Object.cdbz[ll_i] = '1' THEN
						IF Time(ldt_sbsj) <= Time('08:10:00') THEN
							dw_3.Object.cdbz[ll_i] = '0'
						END IF
					END IF
				END IF
				IF ldt_mzsj > ldt_xbsj THEN
					ldt_xbsj = gf_relativedatetime('MINUTE',ldt_mzsj,-2)
					IF dw_3.Object.ztbz[ll_i] = '1' THEN
						IF Time(ldt_xbsj) >= Time('11:20:00') THEN
							dw_3.Object.ztbz[ll_i] = '0'
						END IF
					END IF
				END IF
			END IF
			IF ls_bclb = '下午' THEN
				IF ldt_szsj < ldt_sbsj THEN
					ldt_sbsj = gf_relativedatetime('MINUTE',ldt_szsj,2)
					IF dw_3.Object.cdbz[ll_i] = '1' THEN
						IF Time(ldt_sbsj) <= Time('13:40:00') THEN
							dw_3.Object.cdbz[ll_i] = '0'
						END IF
					END IF
				END IF
				IF ldt_mzsj > ldt_xbsj THEN
					ldt_xbsj = gf_relativedatetime('MINUTE',ldt_mzsj,-2)
					IF dw_3.Object.ztbz[ll_i] = '1' THEN
						IF Time(ldt_xbsj) >= Time('16:50:00') THEN
							dw_3.Object.ztbz[ll_i] = '0'
						END IF
					END IF
				END IF
			END IF
		END IF
	NEXT
END IF

IF dw_3.Update( ) = 1 THEN
	gf_commit_transaction(sqlca)
	MessageBox('提示','计算成功！')
ELSE
	gf_rollback_transaction(sqlca)
END IF





end event

date ldt_begin,ldt_end,ld_zzrq
string ls_bc,ls_ysbm,ls_zzlb
long ll_i ,ll_row,ll_count
ldt_begin = Date(Left(em_1.Text,10))
ldt_end = Date(Left(em_2.Text,10))
dw_3.settransobject(sqlca)
ll_count =dw_3.retrieve(ldt_begin,ldt_end)
if ll_count > 0 then
	for ll_i =1 to ll_count
		ls_ysbm = dw_3.object.ysbm[ll_i]
		ls_bc = dw_3.object.bclb[ll_i]
		ld_zzrq = date(dw_3.object.zzrq[ll_i])
		if ls_bc ='上午' or ls_bc ='下午' then
			if dw_3.object.csrc[ll_i] > 0 then
				select zzlb into :ls_zzlb from mz_pbxx where ysbm = :ls_ysbm and rq =:ld_zzrq and zxbz ='0';
				if ls_zzlb ='2' then
				else
					dw_3.object.csrc[ll_i] = 0
				end if
			end if
		end if
	next 
end if

if dw_3.update( ) = 1 then
	gf_commit_transaction(sqlca)
	messagebox('提示','计算成功！')
else
	gf_rollback_transaction(sqlca)
end if
end event

event clicked;	datetime ldt_begin,ldt_end
	date ldt_rq
	string ls_error,ls_ysbm,ls_bc
	long ll_count_myd,ll_count,ll_i,ll_find
	ldt_begin = DateTime(Date(Left(em_1.Text,10)),Time("00:00:00"))
	ldt_end = DateTime(Date(Left(em_1.Text,10)),Time("23:59:59"))
	u_datastore lds_dxmyd
lds_dxmyd = Create u_datastore
lds_dxmyd.DataObject = 'd_grtj_myd'
lds_dxmyd.SetTransObject(sqlca)

		dw_3.Reset( )
		ll_count_myd = lds_dxmyd.Retrieve('YS',0,ldt_begin,ldt_end )
		//	FOR ll_r = 1 To lds_dxmyd.RowCount()
		//		//		ldc_myd = lds_dxmyd.Object.jhpl[ll_r]
		//		ll_hprs = lds_dxmyd.Object.hps[ll_r]
		//		ll_cprs = lds_dxmyd.Object.cps[ll_r]
		//		ll_cgrs = lds_dxmyd.Object.cgrc[ll_r]
		//		ls_ysbm = lds_dxmyd.Object.zsm[ll_r]
		//		UPDATE mz_gzltj Set hprs = :ll_hprs,cprs = :ll_cprs,cgrs = :ll_cgrs Where zzrq = :ldt_day And ysbm = :ls_ysbm Using sqlca;
		//		if sqlca.sqlcode <> 0 then goto error
		//	NEXT
		ll_count = dw_3.Retrieve(Date(ldt_begin),Date(ldt_begin))
		FOR ll_i = 1 To ll_count
			ls_ysbm = dw_3.Object.ysbm[ll_i]
			ll_find = lds_dxmyd.Find( "zsm ='" + ls_ysbm + "'",1, ll_count_myd)
			IF ll_find > 0 THEN
				dw_3.Object.cgrs[ll_i] = lds_dxmyd.Object.cgrc[ll_find]
				dw_3.Object.cprs[ll_i] = lds_dxmyd.Object.cps[ll_find]
				dw_3.Object.hprs[ll_i] = lds_dxmyd.Object.hps[ll_find]
			ELSE
				dw_3.Object.cgrs[ll_i] = 0
				dw_3.Object.cprs[ll_i] = 0
				dw_3.Object.hprs[ll_i] = 0
			END IF
		NEXT
		FOR ll_i = 1 To ll_count
			IF dw_3.Object.cgrs[ll_i] > 0 Or dw_3.Object.cprs[ll_i] > 0 Or dw_3.Object.hprs[ll_i] > 0 THEN
				IF ldt_rq = Date(dw_3.Object.zzrq[ll_i]) And ls_ysbm = dw_3.Object.ysbm[ll_i]  THEN
					dw_3.Object.cgrs[ll_i] = 0
					dw_3.Object.cprs[ll_i] = 0
					dw_3.Object.hprs[ll_i] = 0
				ELSE
					ls_ysbm = dw_3.Object.ysbm[ll_i]
					ls_bc = dw_3.Object.bclb[ll_i]
					ldt_rq = Date(dw_3.Object.zzrq[ll_i])
				END IF
			ELSE
				dw_3.Object.cgrs[ll_i] = 0
				dw_3.Object.cprs[ll_i] = 0
				dw_3.Object.hprs[ll_i] = 0
			END IF
		NEXT
		
		IF dw_3.Update( ) = 1 THEN
				gf_commit_transaction(sqlca)
				gf_applog('满意度成功！')
			//	Close(Parent)
			//	MessageBox('提示','统计成功！' +String(Left(em_1.Text,10))+ ',' +String(Left(em_2.Text,10)) )
		ELSE
			ls_error = ls_error + sqlca.SQLErrText
			gf_rollback_transaction(sqlca)
			gf_applog('满意度统计失败：' +ls_error )
		END IF


end event

FROM MZ_JZXX,MZ_GHXX
